# BATA Frame Selection Task Tracker - 2026-05-21

Last updated: 2026-05-29T14:48:10+08:00

This is the execution tracker for the Boundary-Aware Token Acquisition input-side route. It is separate from the Sparse TAD model-side tracker. Update this file after every planning change, implementation, review, staging action, diagnostic dump, training launch, evaluation, crash, stop/continue decision, or result interpretation related to intelligent frame/token selection.

## Route Contract

- Topic: intelligent frame/token selection for THUMOS14 TAD.
- Method name: Boundary-Aware Token Acquisition, BATA.
- Updated paper-facing route name: Reasoning-guided Boundary Evidence Acquisition for Cost-aware TAD, RBEA-TAD.
- Allocator name: Boundary-Coverage Allocator, BCA.
- Upgraded allocator concept: boundary evidence triplets + event/context anchors + coverage repair + evidence ledger.
- Mechanism correction: do not distill long video-large-model CoT or agent traces into MobileNet. Use predict-verify local temporal decisions: boundary hypothesis, transition surprise, neighboring candidate comparison, before/transition/after evidence, and coverage verification.
- Final direction: dynamic frame sampling under a fair average-budget constraint.
- Fixed 50% high-cost input tokens remain a fairness anchor, protocol gate, and fallback ablation, not the final ceiling.
- Final target: use a dynamic selected-frame count to approach or exceed the original full-frame AdaTAD result while using substantially fewer high-cost frames.
- Early experiments may use fixed hyperparameters for budget bins and score thresholds; later method versions should learn or adapt the budget.
- Execution correction: first implement and validate a fixed-50% boundary-aware selector. Only after strong fixed-50% performance should we run dynamic-frame-count ablations or extensions.
- Test-time GT: forbidden for deployable runs.
- Test-time teacher predictions: forbidden for deployable runs.
- Train-split GT boundary supervision: allowed only for training a deployable selector and must not leak into val/test score caches.
- Detector/backbone/head/loss/assignment/post-processing: unchanged for input-side attribution, except required coordinate protocol validation/repair.
- Oracle/noisy-oracle runs: diagnostics only, labeled `oracle_diagnostic`, excluded from deployable main tables.
- Dynamic budget is part of the intended final method, but must be defended with matched-average-budget controls.
- Pre-experiments are capped at one offline diagnostic and one minimal detector closed loop before full selector training.
- Do not use a video large model at test time. R1/VLM-style reasoning ideas may be used only as training-time structure, diagnostics, or preference/evidence supervision.
- New large-model-facing branch: query-free Video-LLM TAD via boundary evidence is a deferred enhancement/application line. Direct Video-LLM TAD is only a diagnostic baseline; proposal-conditioned Video-LLM verification requires a separate costed pilot and must not be mixed into the main efficient-TAD table.
- Latest route refinement: two-stage proposal-conditioned Video-LLM verification is also only diagnostic/transitional. The stronger paper-facing route is `Boundary Evidence Acquisition for Temporal Action Tokens`: represent TAD instances as action/time/evidence tokens, and implement the first lightweight version through the RBEA evidence ledger before considering full Video-LLM TAD-token training.
- User correction: avoid step-by-step diagnostic GPU experiments for the TAD-token branch. Before any GPU-heavy pivot, run only idea review, novelty check, Pro discussion, and then a single maximum-information-gain discriminator experiment if justified.
- Pro novelty verdict: `DO NOT PIVOT`. Full Video-LLM TAD-token SFT/RLVR novelty is `3.5/10`; RBEA/BATA with cost-aware boundary evidence acquisition + temporal action-token evidence ledger is about `7/10`. The main route is now fixed as `Boundary Evidence Acquisition for Temporal Action Tokens`.
- New creative native Video-LLM query-free TAD branch verdict: `NEXT-PAPER ONLY`. The strongest future route is not a current pivot, but a benchmark/protocol named `Query-Free Dense Temporal Action Grounding`: evaluate Video-LLMs on dense action-set construction failures such as miss, duplicate, split/merge, calibration, and high-IoU boundary drift, with a minimal set-level post-training recipe. This does not change the current RBEA/BATA implementation path.
- Implementation freeze verdict: start PR-0/PR-1/PR-2 now. First runnable path is score-cache-driven fixed-50 BCA, not MobileNet. BCA v1 uses top-8 NMS boundary peaks, fixed `p-4/p/p+4` triplets, and largest-gap coverage/context repair. Dynamic lambda, multi-head selector, cheap controls, deletion audit, and MobileNet training are deferred until fixed-50 allocator/ledger diagnostics are healthy.

## Status Legend

- `PLANNED`: documented but no code started.
- `NEXT-CODE`: next implementation target.
- `CODED`: local implementation exists; review gates incomplete.
- `REVIEWING`: self/external review in progress.
- `REVIEW-PASS-LOCAL`: required local external reviews passed; remote Linux smoke/deployment still pending.
- `READY-FOR-COMMIT`: required review gates passed.
- `COMMITTED`: reviewed implementation committed.
- `STAGED`: uploaded/prepared on server.
- `QUEUED-SERIAL`: detached wait screen is active and will launch only after current train processes clear and gates pass.
- `RUNNING`: active train/eval/diagnostic process.
- `DONE`: completed with recorded result or accepted infrastructure.
- `BLOCKED`: cannot proceed until prerequisite resolves.
- `DEFERRED`: intentionally postponed.
- `FAILED-RUN`: launched and failed; no relaunch before diagnosis.

## Implementation Review Gate

For any BATA code implementation:

1. Self-check: changed files, experimental purpose, strict 50% budget, no GT/teacher leakage, tensor/metadata semantics, coordinate protocol, and exact local verification.
2. GPT-5 Pro review: required for major coordinate, selector, allocator, LoadFrames, loss, or launcher changes intended for GPU run.
3. Gemini CLI read-only review with `gemini-3-pro-preview`.
4. Claude CLI DeepSeek read-only verification with `deepseek-v4-pro`.
5. Commit reviewed code before staging or long training.

## Stage Gates

Gate -1 success:

- coordinate unit tests pass;
- branch-hit debug for sparse configs is positive;
- random-fixed clean baseline reaches at least `63.3` Avg-mAP and `41.5@0.7`;
- stratified reference reaches at least `64.0` Avg-mAP and `42.7@0.7`;
- no test-time GT/teacher leakage.

Compressed pre-experiment go:

- Pre-1 shows fixed-50% boundary-aware allocation increases boundary density without exploding coverage gaps;
- Pre-1 selected positions are not just action-interior or high-motion selection;
- Pre-2, the one minimal detector loop, improves mAP@0.7 or gives a clear go/no-go without Avg-mAP collapse.

Simple signal go:

- the one or two selected simple signals reach BDG@4 >= 1.2-1.3 relative to uniform/stratified;
- coverage p95 gap <= 1.5x stratified;
- action-interior and scene/camera false peaks are controllable.

Fixed-50% selector go:

- learned selector offline improves over the best simple signal;
- fixed-50% TAD run preserves no-test-GT/no-teacher and unchanged detector attribution;
- fixed-50% selected-frame count is verified in diagnostics.
- dynamic frame count starts after fixed-50% performance is usable and diagnostics are healthy; do not require a broad fixed-50% ablation block first.

Paper success:

- target reference is full-frame AdaTAD `68.97 / 47.46@0.7`;
- first fixed-50% goal: beat stratified `64.64 / 43.23@0.7`;
- stronger fixed-50% goal: reach `65.5+` Avg-mAP and clearly improve mAP@0.7;
- later dynamic goal: after fixed-50% works, `25% / 50% / 75%` bins average around `40%-45%` high-cost frames while preserving most fixed-50% learned-selector performance;
- BDG@4 improves;
- coverage p95 gap does not explode;
- detector/backbone/head unchanged;
- main table excludes oracle/teacher diagnostics.
- fixed-50% main table reports preview compute/I/O separately; dynamic省帧 tables use high-cost frames plus preview-equivalent cost.
- MobileNet boundary learning quality and selected high-cost frame distributions are mandatory diagnostics.
- fixed-50% ablations are not the next objective; dynamic frame count is the next method step after fixed-50% validation.

## Mandatory Update Protocol

For every BATA event, update:

1. this tracker;
2. `research-wiki/log.md`;
3. the route-specific plan/report if the event changes the route decision;
4. the sparse tracker only when the event affects shared protocol, server allocation, or global experiment scheduling.

Exception for ongoing `grill-me`: complete per-question records are kept only in `BATA_GRILL_ME_QA_20260521.md`. Do not update this tracker or `research-wiki/log.md` after every single question. After the full `grill-me` task is complete, add one compact stage summary here and in `research-wiki/log.md`.

Every update must record:

- timestamp with timezone;
- changed files or commit if code changed;
- server/screen/log/config if any remote action occurs;
- fixed-budget or dynamic-budget status and average budget;
- GT/teacher leakage status;
- current mAP evidence if any;
- next launch/continue/stop decision.
